## Component and subsumption boundaries

Component fixtures point directly at existing canonical schema nodes through their `definition` JSON pointer. They execute through the installed shape validator. They are explicitly component evidence, not a claim that an invalid primitive is a complete valid event. Added positive counterparts exercise those same nodes; full-object conditional controls remain separate. No schema is copied or relaxed.

Review root minimum=1 is subsumed in a full payload by single-pass const=1 or multi-pass minimum=2, so its independent witness is the actual property component. The checkpoint list has 18 enum members and uniqueItems, which imply maxItems=18; its former 257-invalid-member length specimen is replaced by an isolated enum specimen, and a two-element duplicate specimen isolates uniqueness. The shared tool must inventory that implied length site rather than merge length, uniqueness and membership. For fixed-width LF cases, the character specimen retains the original width, while the length specimen appends only a valid character. The focused width check removes each maximum alone, then both, relaxing only the relevant final regex width bound and preserving its opposite bound, alphabet, prefix/groups and anchors. The same site-by-site check applies to minimum-width pairs. Structurally implied reference minima are retained as shared-tool inventory qualifications. Null source bodies also activate required/not selectors vacuously; those six composite specimens now exercise the actual forbidden-member then-node with an otherwise valid object. Classification of the subsumed outer type sites belongs to SW-P1.

## Application generator inventory

Old nested IDs are `source_case:r2:app_context:index:field`; new IDs append `:nested_join_only`. Old wrapper IDs are `source_case:r2:wrapper_identity`; new IDs append `:outer_server_only`. The source-only older `:app_context` probes remain and still run.

| Old ID | New ID | Target rule |
|---|---|---|
| `source_collaboration_created_application:r2:app_context:0:thread_id` | `source_collaboration_created_application:r2:app_context:0:thread_id:nested_join_only` | nested thread_id join to original holder/event |

## Other generator repairs

Empty/nested context and policy objects are replaced by complete valid peers with one member removed. Token schemas change only one clause. Wrapper-version negatives are explicitly schema components; full-path positives and original-record equality negatives stay separate.

| Old ID | Final IDs | Target rule |
|---|---|---|
| `read_token:string` | `read_token:string:type_only` | token schema root type only |
| `read_token:arbitrary` | `read_token:arbitrary:required_member_only` | token required storage_instance_id only |
| `source_collaboration_created_application:r2:wrapper_record_schema` | `source_collaboration_created_application:r2:wrapper_record_schema:component` | record schema_version constant component; original-record join is outside this specimen |
| `source_collaboration_started_application:r2:wrapper_record_schema` | `source_collaboration_started_application:r2:wrapper_record_schema:component` | record schema_version constant component; original-record join is outside this specimen |
